## Call 1

```python
import numpy as np
print(sorted(W.keys()))
for k in sorted(W.keys()):
    a = W[k]
    print(k, a.shape, a.dtype, "mean %.4f std %.4f min %.4f max %.4f" % (a.mean(), a.std(), a.min(), a.max()))

np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=150)
for l in range(4):
    print("=== layer", l)
    print("ln1_w: mean %.3f std %.3f; ln1_b std %.3f" % (W[f'ln1_w.{l}'].mean(), W[f'ln1_w.{l}'].std(), W[f'ln1_b.{l}'].std()))
    print("ln2_w: mean %.3f std %.3f; ln2_b std %.3f" % (W[f'ln2_w.{l}'].mean(), W[f'ln2_w.{l}'].std(), W[f'ln2_b.{l}'].std()))
    for name in ['W_q','W_k','W_v','W_o','W_fc','W_proj']:
        M = W[f'{name}.{l}']
        s = np.linalg.svd(M, compute_uv=False)
        print(name, "fro %.2f" % np.linalg.norm(M), "top sv", s[:8], "sv[50] %.3f sv[200] %.3f min %.3f" % (s[50], s[200], s[-1]))
    for j in range(6):
        s = slice(64*j, 64*(j+1))
        Q, K, V = W[f'W_q.{l}'][s], W[f'W_k.{l}'][s], W[f'W_v.{l}'][s]
        O = W[f'W_o.{l}'][:, s]
        G = Q.T @ K
        print(f"H{j}: |Q| {np.linalg.norm(Q):.2f} |K| {np.linalg.norm(K):.2f} |V| {np.linalg.norm(V):.2f} |O| {np.linalg.norm(O):.2f} "
              f"|QtK| {np.linalg.norm(G):.2f} trace {np.trace(G):.2f} sym {np.linalg.norm((G+G.T)/2):.2f} anti {np.linalg.norm((G-G.T)/2):.2f}")

```

## Call 2

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
def sv(M): return np.linalg.svd(M, compute_uv=False)
print("W_proj.3 sv 0..60"); print(sv(W['W_proj.3'])[:60])
print("W_o.3 sv 0..60"); print(sv(W['W_o.3'])[:60])
print("W_v.3 sv 0..60"); print(sv(W['W_v.3'])[:60])
print("W_q.0 sv 0..200 step"); print(sv(W['W_q.0'])[:200:4])
print("W_k.0 sv"); print(sv(W['W_k.0'])[:200:4])
print("W_fc.0 sv"); print(sv(W['W_fc.0'])[:200:4])
print("W_proj.0 sv"); print(sv(W['W_proj.0'])[:200:4])

# alignment of dominant key directions across layers
U={}
for name in ['W_q','W_k','W_v','W_o','W_fc','W_proj']:
    for l in range(4):
        U[name,l]=np.linalg.svd(W[f'{name}.{l}'],full_matrices=False)
def right(name,l,k=2): return U[name,l][2][:k].T
def left(name,l,k=2): return U[name,l][0][:,:k]
print('--- subspace overlaps of top2 key right-singular dirs among layers')
for l in range(4):
    for m in range(4):
        print(l,m, np.linalg.svd(right('W_k',l).T@right('W_k',m),compute_uv=False), end=' | ')
    print()
print('--- W_k.l right vs W_o.(l-1) left top2, W_proj.(l-1) left top4')
for l in range(1,4):
    print(l,'o',np.linalg.svd(right('W_k',l).T@left('W_o',l-1,4),compute_uv=False),'proj',np.linalg.svd(right('W_k',l).T@left('W_proj',l-1,4),compute_uv=False))
print('--- per-head loadings of top 4 left singular vectors of W_k (energy per head)')
for l in range(4):
    u=U['W_k',l][0]; s=U['W_k',l][1]
    for i in range(4):
        print(l,i,'s=%.2f'%s[i],[round(float((u[64*j:64*j+64,i]**2).sum()),3) for j in range(6)])
print('--- per-head loadings of top 4 right singular vectors of W_o (energy per head)')
for l in range(4):
    v=U['W_o',l][2]; s=U['W_o',l][1]
    for i in range(4):
        print(l,i,'s=%.2f'%s[i],[round(float((v[i,64*j:64*j+64]**2).sum()),3) for j in range(6)])

## Call 3

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
D=np.diag(W['ln1_w.0'])
print("ln1_w.0 sorted top30", np.sort(W['ln1_w.0'])[::-1][:30], "n>0.5", (W['ln1_w.0']>0.5).sum(), "n<0.1",(W['ln1_w.0']<0.1).sum())
print("ln2_w.0 hist", np.histogram(W['ln2_w.0'],bins=8))
print("ln1_b.1 sorted abs top", np.sort(np.abs(W['ln1_b.1']))[::-1][:12])
print("ln1_b.2 sorted abs top", np.sort(np.abs(W['ln1_b.2']))[::-1][:12])
print("ln1_b.3 sorted abs top", np.sort(np.abs(W['ln1_b.3']))[::-1][:12])
for l in range(4):
    Dl=np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}'])
    print("=== Layer",l)
    for j in range(6):
        s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
        Q=W[f'W_q.{l}'][s]@Dl; K=W[f'W_k.{l}'][s]@Dl; V=W[f'W_v.{l}'][s]@Dl
        A=Q.T@K/8
        sa=np.linalg.svd(A,compute_uv=False)
        ev=np.linalg.eigvals(A)
        idx=np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))
        ev=ev[idx]
        print(f" H{j}: sv(A) {sa[:6]} |  svQ {np.linalg.svd(Q,compute_uv=False)[:4]} svK {np.linalg.svd(K,compute_uv=False)[:4]} svV {np.linalg.svd(V,compute_uv=False)[:4]}")
        print("     top eig mag", np.abs(ev[:10]), "phase(deg)", np.degrees(np.angle(ev[:10])).round(0))
        print("     tr(A)=%.2f  fro=%.2f  frac eig real>0 weighted %.2f" % (np.trace(A), np.linalg.norm(A), (ev.real[ev.real>0].sum())/np.abs(ev.real).sum()))

import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_o.0'],full_matrices=False)
for i in range(4):
    u=U[:,i]; idx=np.argsort(-np.abs(u))[:6]
    print('W_o.0 left',i,'s=%.2f'%S[i],'top coords',idx,u[idx].round(3),'energy top3 %.3f'%(np.sort(u**2)[::-1][:3].sum()))
for l in range(1,4):
    U2,S2,Vt2=np.linalg.svd(W[f'W_k.{l}'],full_matrices=False)
    for i in range(3):
        v=Vt2[i]; idx=np.argsort(-np.abs(v))[:6]
        print(f'W_k.{l} right',i,'s=%.2f'%S2[i],'top coords',idx,v[idx].round(3))
# dims with big bias
for l in range(1,4):
    b=W[f'ln1_b.{l}']; idx=np.argsort(-np.abs(b))[:8]
    print('ln1_b',l,idx,b[idx].round(3),'ln1_w there',W[f'ln1_w.{l}'][idx].round(3))
for l in range(4):
    w=W[f'ln2_w.{l}']; idx=np.argsort(w)[:8]
    print('ln2_w smallest',l,idx,w[idx].round(3),'ln2_b there',W[f'ln2_b.{l}'][idx].round(3))
for l in range(4):
    w=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']; idx=np.argsort(w)[:8]
    print('ln1_w smallest',l,idx,w[idx].round(3))
# big entries in W_o and W_proj, W_fc
for name in ['W_o','W_proj','W_fc','W_k','W_q']:
    for l in range(4):
        M=W[f'{name}.{l}']
        rown=np.linalg.norm(M,axis=1); coln=np.linalg.norm(M,axis=0)
        ri=np.argsort(-rown)[:6]; ci=np.argsort(-coln)[:6]
        print(name,l,'rows',ri,rown[ri].round(2),'median %.2f'%np.median(rown),'cols',ci,coln[ci].round(2),'median %.2f'%np.median(coln))

## Call 5

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
Dv=W['ln1_w.0']; D=np.diag(Dv)
def head(M,j): return M[64*j:64*j+64]
sp={}
for j in range(6):
    Q=head(W['W_q.0'],j)@D; K=head(W['W_k.0'],j)@D; V=head(W['W_v.0'],j)@D; O=W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
    print(f'H{j} svQ',np.linalg.svd(Q,compute_uv=False)[:40:3])
    print(f'H{j} svK',np.linalg.svd(K,compute_uv=False)[:40:3])
    print(f'H{j} svV',np.linalg.svd(V,compute_uv=False)[:40:3])
    print(f'H{j} svO',np.linalg.svd(O,compute_uv=False)[:40:3])
    print(f'H{j} svOV',np.linalg.svd(O@V,compute_uv=False)[:40:3])
    sp[j]=(np.linalg.svd(Q)[2],np.linalg.svd(K)[2],np.linalg.svd(V)[2],np.linalg.svd(O)[0])
print()
# overlap between Q,K,V input subspaces (D-weighted) of head 3 & others: sum of squared cosines
def ov(A,B): return np.linalg.svd(A.T@B,compute_uv=False)
for (a,ka),(b,kb) in [((3,0),(3,1)),((3,0),(3,2)),((3,1),(3,2))]:
    A=sp[a][ka][:20].T; B=sp[b][kb][:20].T
    print('H3 subspaces',ka,kb,'cos top',ov(A,B)[:20].round(2))
A=sp[3][2][:28].T
for j in [0,1,2,4,5]:
    print('V3 top28 vs Q,K,V of',j,[ov(A,sp[j][k][:6].T)[:6].round(2) for k in range(3)])
# global input subspace: stack DW_q,DW_k,DW_v,W_fc
stack=np.vstack([W['W_q.0']@D,W['W_k.0']@D,W['W_v.0']@D])
s=np.linalg.svd(stack,compute_uv=False)
print('stack sv',s[:80:2])
# W_fc.0 row-space vs W_o.0 range
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(W['W_o.0']); Uf,Sf,Vf=np.linalg.svd(W['W_fc.0'],full_matrices=False)
Dn=np.diag(W['ln2_w.0'])
Fe=W['W_fc.0']@Dn
Sfe=np.linalg.svd(Fe,compute_uv=False)
print('W_fc.0 D2 sv',Sfe[:80:3])
Ustack=np.linalg.svd(stack,full_matrices=False)[2]
Vfe=np.linalg.svd(Fe,full_matrices=False)[2]
print('FC top 28 vs stack top 28 cos',ov(Vfe[:28].T,Ustack[:28].T).round(2))
print('FC top 28..60 vs stack top 28 cos',ov(Vfe[28:60].T,Ustack[:28].T).round(2))
print('FC top 60 vs Wo-H3 left top 28',ov(Vfe[:60].T,sp[3][3][:,:28]).round(2))
print('FC top 60 vs stack top 60',ov(Vfe[:60].T,Ustack[:60].T).round(2)[:60:3])

## Call 6

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
Dv=W['ln1_w.0']; D=np.diag(Dv)
def head(M,j): return M[64*j:64*j+64]
tops={}
for j in range(6):
    Q=head(W['W_q.0'],j)@D; K=head(W['W_k.0'],j)@D; V=head(W['W_v.0'],j)@D; O=W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64]
    uq,sq,vq=np.linalg.svd(Q,full_matrices=False)
    uk,sk,vk=np.linalg.svd(K,full_matrices=False)
    uv,sv,vv=np.linalg.svd(V,full_matrices=False)
    OV=O@V
    uo,so,vo=np.linalg.svd(OV,full_matrices=False)
    tops[j]=dict(q=vq[0],k=vk[0],v=vv[0],ov=vo[0],out=uo[:,0], q2=vq[1],k2=vk[1],v2=vv[1], uq=uq[:,0],uk=uk[:,0])
    print(f'H{j}: q0 top coords',np.argsort(-np.abs(vq[0]))[:6],'k0',np.argsort(-np.abs(vk[0]))[:6],'ov0',np.argsort(-np.abs(vo[0]))[:6],'out',np.argsort(-np.abs(uo[:,0]))[:6], uo[:,0][np.argsort(-np.abs(uo[:,0]))[:6]].round(2))
    print('    uq.uk %.3f (q-space vs k-space alignment of top dirs)'%(uq[:,0]@uk[:,0]))
keys=['q','k','ov','q2','k2','v2']
names=[f'{k}{j}' for j in range(6) for k in keys]
M=np.array([tops[j][k] for j in range(6) for k in keys])
C=M@M.T
import pandas as pd
pd.set_option('display.width',250); pd.set_option('display.max_columns',50)
print(pd.DataFrame(C,index=names,columns=names).round(2).to_string())
# A eigenvalues for H3 and H0 full listing
for j in [3,0]:
    Q=head(W['W_q.0'],j)@D; K=head(W['W_k.0'],j)@D
    A=Q.T@K/8
    ev=np.linalg.eigvals(A); ev=ev[np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))]
    pos=ev[ev.imag>=0]
    print('head',j,'eig (mag,phase) upper half-plane')
    print(np.c_[np.abs(pos)[:36],np.degrees(np.angle(pos))[:36]].T.round(2))

```

## Call 7

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=190)
P=W['W_proj.3']; F=W['W_fc.3']
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P,full_matrices=False)
for r in [24,28,30]:
    C=(U[:,:r].T@P).T   # neurons x r
    print('r',r,'kurtosis of raw comps',np.round(((C-C.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(C.var(0)**2)-3,1))
r=28
C=(U[:,:r].T@P).T
ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=2000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0)
Sx=ica.fit_transform(C)
kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
print('ICA kurt',np.round(kur,1))
skew=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**3).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**1.5)
print('ICA skew',np.round(skew,1))
A=ica.mixing_   # r x r, columns are axes in SVD coords
print('axis norms (mixing)',np.round(np.linalg.norm(A,axis=0),2))
# orient to positive skew
sign=np.sign(skew); Sx2=Sx*sign; A2=A*sign
order=np.argsort(-np.linalg.norm(A2,axis=0)); 
print('sorted norms',np.round(np.linalg.norm(A2,axis=0)[order],2))
print('top neuron loads per axis (max, min)',np.round(Sx2[:,order].max(0),1),np.round(Sx2[:,order].min(0),1))
# neuron co-loading between axes
Sn=Sx2[:,order]
Corr=np.corrcoef(Sn.T)
print('max offdiag corr',np.abs(Corr-np.eye(r)).max())
Pos=np.clip(Sn-1.5,0,None); 
co=(Pos.T@Pos); d=np.sqrt(np.diag(co)); co=co/np.outer(d,d)
print(np.round(co,1))

```

import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=3, suppress=True, linewidth=170)
def head(M,j): return M[64*j:64*j+64]
Dw=[np.diag(W[f'ln1_w.{l}']) for l in range(4)]
Q3=head(W['W_q.0'],3)@Dw[0]; K3=head(W['W_k.0'],3)@Dw[0]; V3=head(W['W_v.0'],3)@Dw[0]; O3=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
st=np.vstack([Q3,K3])
Us,Ss,Vs=np.linalg.svd(st,full_matrices=False)
print('Q3+K3 stack sv',Ss[:64:2])
for r in [12,20,30,40]:
    P=Vs[:r].T
    print('--- r',r)
    for l in range(4):
        row=[]
        for j in range(6):
            Q=head(W[f'W_q.{l}'],j)@Dw[l]; K=head(W[f'W_k.{l}'],j)@Dw[l]; V=head(W[f'W_v.{l}'],j)@Dw[l]
            row.append([np.linalg.norm(Q@P)/np.linalg.norm(Q),np.linalg.norm(K@P)/np.linalg.norm(K),np.linalg.norm(V@P)/np.linalg.norm(V)])
        print(l,np.array(row).round(2).reshape(-1))
# overlap of same subspace with W_fc rows & W_v.0 heads
Fe=W['W_fc.0']@np.diag(W['ln2_w.0'])
print('FC.0 energy frac in P(40)',np.linalg.norm(Fe@Vs[:40].T)/np.linalg.norm(Fe))
Fv=np.linalg.svd(Fe,full_matrices=False)[2]
print('cos FC top60 / P40',np.linalg.svd(Fv[:60]@Vs[:40].T,compute_uv=False)[:20].round(2))
# V3 detail
print('O3 V3 svs',np.linalg.svd(O3@V3,compute_uv=False)[:40].round(3))
print('V3 svs',np.linalg.svd(V3,compute_uv=False)[:40].round(3))

## Call 9

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=190)
def head(M,j): return M[64*j:64*j+64]
D0=np.diag(W['ln1_w.0'])
Qs=[head(W['W_q.0'],j)@D0 for j in range(6)]; Ks=[head(W['W_k.0'],j)@D0 for j in range(6)]
As=[Qs[j].T@Ks[j]/8 for j in range(6)]
st=np.vstack([Qs[3],Ks[3]]); Vs=np.linalg.svd(st,full_matrices=False)[2]
r=44
P=Vs[:r].T
A3=P.T@As[3]@P
lam,R=np.linalg.eig(A3); L=np.linalg.inv(R)
idx=np.argsort(-np.abs(lam))
print('eig mag',np.abs(lam[idx])[:44:2]); print('phase',np.degrees(np.angle(lam[idx]))[:44:2].round(0))
sel=[i for i in idx if lam[i].imag>1e-6][:16]
print('plane   lam3mag  phase3 | for head h: |z| phase')
for i in sel:
    row=[abs(lam[i]),np.degrees(np.angle(lam[i]))]
    for h in [0,1,2,4,5]:
        Ah=P.T@As[h]@P
        z=(L[i,:]@Ah@R[:,i])
        row+= [abs(z),np.degrees(np.angle(z))]
    print(np.array(row).round(3))
# shift-operator: S=pinv(K3 P) Q3 P
Qp=Qs[3]@P; Kp=Ks[3]@P
for rc in [1e-1,3e-1]:
    S=np.linalg.pinv(Kp,rcond=rc)@Qp
    ev=np.linalg.eigvals(S); ev=ev[np.argsort(-np.abs(ev))]; ev=ev[ev.imag>=0]
    print('rcond',rc,'S eig mag',np.abs(ev)[:24]); print('   phase',np.degrees(np.angle(ev))[:24].round(0))
# angles between Q and K: polar rotation in the 64-d head space
U,s,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Qs[3]@Ks[3].T)
Rr=U@Vt
ev=np.linalg.eigvals(Rr); ev=ev[ev.imag>=0]
print('polar R(Q K^T) phases',np.sort(np.degrees(np.angle(ev))).round(0))

```

import numpy as np

D2=W['ln2_w.0']
F=W['W_fc.0']*D2   # rows in x-hat coordinates of stream (weighted)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']
print('neuron row norm pct',np.percentile(np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1),[1,10,50,90,99,99.9]))
print('proj col norm pct',np.percentile(np.linalg.norm(W['W_proj.0'],axis=0),[1,10,50,90,99,99.9]))
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(W['W_proj.0'],axis=0)
print('product pct',np.percentile(nr,[10,50,90,99,99.9]), 'n strong (>1)',(nr>1).sum(),'(>0.5)',(nr>0.5).sum())
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Fraw,full_matrices=False)
print(S[:70:3])
for r in [40,56,64]:
    C=U[:,:r]*S[:r]
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=0)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(C)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    skew=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**3).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**1.5)
    print('r',r,'kurt sorted',np.sort(kur)[::-1].round(1))
    print('    skew',skew[np.argsort(-kur)].round(1))

## Call 11

```python
import numpy as np
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=190)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
print(F.shape)
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(F,full_matrices=False)
print('sv strong rows',S[:80:2].round(2))
# Components of O (H3) range
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
D2=W['ln2_w.0']
for r in [44,52,56,60]:
    C=(F@Vt[:r].T)
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=5000,tol=1e-6,random_state=1)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(C)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    print('r',r,'kurt sorted',np.sort(kur)[::-1].round(1))
    # direction in 384 space: axis (mixing columns)
    Ax=(ica.mixing_.T@Vt[:r])  # each row: a 384-vector
    Ax=Ax/np.linalg.norm(Ax,axis=1,keepdims=True)
    # scale: functional in stream x: multiply by ln2_w
    Axw=Ax*D2
    frac=[np.linalg.norm(Uo[:,:24].T@(a/np.linalg.norm(a))) for a in Axw]
    frac2=[np.linalg.norm(Uo[:,:24].T@(a/np.linalg.norm(a))) for a in Ax]
    o=np.argsort(-kur)
    print('   frac in O3 range (D-weighted)',np.array(frac)[o].round(2))
    # how many neurons load strongly on each comp, and how many comps per neuron
    Z=Sx/Sx.std(0)
    print('   n neurons |z|>3 per comp',(np.abs(Z)>3).sum(0)[o])
    print('   comps per neuron (|z|>3) hist',np.bincount((np.abs(Z)>3).sum(1)))

import numpy as np, warnings

from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=190)

    Gp=G@B@B.T; Gc=G-Gp

## Call 13

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=190)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
G=Fraw[idx]*D2
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B=Uo[:,:28]
Gp=G@B; Gc=G-G@B@B.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Gc,full_matrices=False)
Upp,Spp,Vpp=np.linalg.svd(Gp,full_matrices=False)
res={}
for name,(M,rs) in {'cur':(Gc@Vcc.T,[26,28,30,32,34,38]),'prev':(Gp@Vpp.T,[16,18,20,22])}.items():
    for r in rs:
        best=None
        for seed in range(3):
            ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed,fun='cube')
            Sx=ica.fit_transform(M[:,:r])
            kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
            if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx)
        kur=best[1]; Sx=best[2]
        skew=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**3).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**1.5)
        o=np.argsort(-kur)
        print(name,'r',r,'kurt',kur[o].round(0))
        print('      skew',skew[o].round(1))
        Z=Sx/Sx.std(0)*np.sign(skew)
        print('      n(z>3)',(Z>3).sum(0)[o],'n(z<-3)',(Z<-3).sum(0)[o])

import numpy as np, warnings, pickle
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]; Vs=W['W_v.0'][64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fp=F@B2@B2.T; Fc=F-Fp
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
Upp,Spp,Vpp=np.linalg.svd(Fp,full_matrices=False)
print('Spp',Spp[:24].round(2)); print('Scc',Scc[:36].round(2))
def run(M,r,seeds=4):
    best=None
    for seed in range(seeds):
        ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
        Sx=ica.fit_transform(M[:,:r])
        kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
        if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
    return best
rc=30; rp=20
_,kc,Sc,Mc=run(Fc@Vcc.T,rc)
_,kp,Sp,Mp=run(Fp@Vpp.T,rp)
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); sp=np.sign(((Sp-Sp.mean(0))**3).mean(0))
Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc; Sp=Sp*sp; Mp=Mp*sp
oc=np.argsort(-kc); op=np.argsort(-kp)
Sc,Mc,kc=Sc[:,oc],Mc[:,oc],kc[oc]; Sp,Mp,kp=Sp[:,op],Mp[:,op],kp[op]
Ac=(Mc.T@Vcc[:rc]); Ap=(Mp.T@Vpp[:rp])  # axes in raw row coordinates: rows k x 384
print('kc',kc.round(0)); print('kp',kp.round(0))
print('n z>3 cur',(Sc>3).sum(0)); print('n z>3 prev',(Sp>3).sum(0)); print('n z<-3 prev',(Sp<-3).sum(0))
# tc estimate: axis / D2 (floor)
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.2)
Tc=Ac/fl
# V(t_c) -> O -> D2  to compare with prev axes
Vt=(Vs*D1)@Tc.T     # 64 x rc
wr=(Os@Vt).T        # rc x 384, written vectors
wr2=wr*D2
# project onto Ap (least squares): coefficient of written vector on prev axes
Co=np.linalg.lstsq(Ap.T,wr2.T,rcond=None)[0].T  # rc x rp
Nc=Co/ (np.abs(Co).max(1,keepdims=True)+1e-9)
print('mapping cur->prev normalised'); print(Nc.round(1))
print('argmax cur->prev',np.abs(Co).argmax(1)); 
# co-activation
Zc=np.clip(Sc-1.5,0,None); Zp=np.clip(Sp-1.5,0,None); Zpn=np.clip(-Sp-1.5,0,None)
T=Zc.T@Zp
print('co-activation (rows cur comp, cols prev comp)'); print(T.round(0).astype(int))
np.savez('/tmp/ica0.npz',Sc=Sc,Sp=Sp,Ac=Ac,Ap=Ap,Co=Co,idx=idx,kc=kc,kp=kp)

## Call 15

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=230)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]; Vs=W['W_v.0'][64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B=Uo[:,:28]
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(F,full_matrices=False)
r=54
best=None
for seed in range(5):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=4000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(F@Vt[:r].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kur,Sx,Mx=best
skew=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**3).mean(0)
sg=np.sign(skew); Sx=Sx*sg; Mx=Mx*sg
Ax=(Mx.T@Vt[:r])              # k x 384 in raw row coordinates (=~ D2 * input vector)
nrm=np.linalg.norm(Ax,axis=1)
Axu=Ax/nrm[:,None]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15)
Xv=Ax/fl                      # estimated stream vector for that component
frac=np.linalg.norm(Xv@B,axis=1)/np.linalg.norm(Xv,axis=1)
fracw=np.linalg.norm((Ax*D2)@B,axis=1)/np.linalg.norm(Ax*D2,axis=1)
isprev=fracw>0.6
print('n prev',isprev.sum(),'n cur',(~isprev).sum())
print('fracw sorted',np.sort(fracw).round(2))
cur=np.where(~isprev)[0]; prv=np.where(isprev)[0]
print('kurt cur',kur[cur].round(0)); print('n z>3 cur',(Sx[:,cur]>3).sum(0)); print('kurt prev',kur[prv].round(0)); print('n z>3 prev',(Sx[:,prv]>3).sum(0))
# mapping cur->prev through V,O
Vc=(Vs*D1)@Xv[cur].T
wr=(Os@Vc).T*D2
Co=np.linalg.lstsq(Ax[prv].T,wr.T,rcond=None)[0].T
# scale
Co=Co
print('map max/second per cur:')
aa=np.abs(Co); o=np.argsort(-aa,axis=1)
print('argmax',prv[o[:,0]] )
print('ratio',(aa[np.arange(len(cur)),o[:,0]]/aa[np.arange(len(cur)),o[:,1]]).round(1))
print('value',Co[np.arange(len(cur)),o[:,0]].round(2))
# co activation on combined comps
Z=np.clip(Sx-2,0,None)
Tab=Z[:,cur].T@Z[:,prv]
print('coact cur x prev'); print(Tab.round(0).astype(int))
print('comps per neuron z>2.5 cur',np.bincount((Sx[:,cur]>2.5).sum(1)),'prev',np.bincount((Sx[:,prv]>2.5).sum(1)))

## Call 16

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=230)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
rc=30
best=None
for seed in range(6):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=rc,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fc@Vcc[:rc].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
oc=np.argsort(-kc); Sc,Mc,kc=Sc[:,oc],Mc[:,oc],kc[oc]
Ac=Mc.T@Vcc[:rc]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15)
Tc=Ac/fl
Tn=Tc/np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1,keepdims=True)
print('kurt',kc.round(0)); print('n>3',(Sc>3).sum(0)); print('axis norm',np.linalg.norm(Ac,axis=1).round(2))
G=Tn@Tn.T
print('gram offdiag max',np.abs(G-np.eye(rc)).max().round(2),' mean abs',np.abs(G-np.eye(rc)).mean().round(3))
for j in range(6):
    s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
    Q=W['W_q.0'][s]*D1; K=W['W_k.0'][s]*D1; V=W['W_v.0'][s]*D1
    uq,sq,vq=np.linalg.svd(Q,full_matrices=False); uk,sk,vk=np.linalg.svd(K,full_matrices=False); uv,sv,vv=np.linalg.svd(V,full_matrices=False)
    print(f'H{j} q0',(Tn@vq[0]).round(1))
    print(f'H{j} q1',(Tn@vq[1]).round(1))
    print(f'H{j} k0',(Tn@vk[0]).round(1))
    print(f'H{j} k1',(Tn@vk[1]).round(1))
    print(f'H{j} v0',(Tn@vv[0]).round(1))

```

## Call 17

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=230)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
# T-space in x coordinates: axes / D2 ; use top 30 of Fc rows divided by D2
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15)
Tbasis,_=np.linalg.qr((Vcc[:30]/fl).T)
Pst=np.vstack([(W['W_q.0'][64*3:64*3+64]*D1),(W['W_k.0'][64*3:64*3+64]*D1)])
Pb=np.linalg.svd(Pst,full_matrices=False)[2][:30].T
print('T vs P cos',np.linalg.svd(Tbasis.T@Pb,compute_uv=False)[:30].round(2))
for j in range(6):
    s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
    Q=W['W_q.0'][s]*D1; K=W['W_k.0'][s]*D1; V=W['W_v.0'][s]*D1
    O=W['W_o.0'][:,s]
    OV=O@V
    vq=np.linalg.svd(Q,full_matrices=False)[2]; vk=np.linalg.svd(K,full_matrices=False)[2]; vo=np.linalg.svd(OV,full_matrices=False)[2]
    def fr(v,B): return float(np.linalg.norm(B.T@v))
    print(f'H{j}: frac in T / P:  q0 {fr(vq[0],Tbasis):.2f}/{fr(vq[0],Pb):.2f}  q1 {fr(vq[1],Tbasis):.2f}/{fr(vq[1],Pb):.2f}  k0 {fr(vk[0],Tbasis):.2f}/{fr(vk[0],Pb):.2f}  k1 {fr(vk[1],Tbasis):.2f}/{fr(vk[1],Pb):.2f} OV0 {fr(vo[0],Tbasis):.2f}/{fr(vo[0],Pb):.2f} OV1 {fr(vo[1],Tbasis):.2f}/{fr(vo[1],Pb):.2f}')
# Energy of each head's Q,K,V in T / P
for j in range(6):
    s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
    for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
        M=W[f'{nm}.0'][s]*D1
        print(j,nm,'T %.2f  P %.2f'%(np.linalg.norm(M@Tbasis)/np.linalg.norm(M),np.linalg.norm(M@Pb)/np.linalg.norm(M)),end=' | ')
    print()

## Call 18

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=1, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]; Vs=W['W_v.0'][64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
rc=28
best=None
for seed in range(6):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=rc,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fc@Vcc[:rc].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
oc=np.argsort(-kc); Sc,Mc,kc=Sc[:,oc],Mc[:,oc],kc[oc]
Ac=Mc.T@Vcc[:rc]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15)
Tc=Ac/fl
wp=((Os@((Vs*D1)@Tc.T)).T)*D2     # rc x 384 : prev-channel vector (raw coords) for each comp
print('kurt',kc.round(0)); print('n>3',(Sc>3).sum(0))
print('|wp| rel',(np.linalg.norm(wp,axis=1)/np.linalg.norm(Ac,axis=1)).round(2))
Pm=F@wp.T      # n x rc
Pm=(Pm-Pm.mean(0))/Pm.std(0)
Tab=np.zeros((rc,rc)); 
for c in range(rc):
    sel=np.where(Sc[:,c]>3)[0]
    if len(sel)==0: continue
    w=Sc[sel,c]
    Tab[c]=(Pm[sel]*w[:,None]).sum(0)/w.sum()
print('rows: cur comp; cols: prev comp (weighted mean z of prev-sensitivity of neurons dedicated to cur comp)')
print('    '+' '.join(f'{i:4d}' for i in range(rc)))
for c in range(rc):
    print(f'{c:3d} '+' '.join(f'{v:4.1f}' for v in Tab[c]))
print('diag',np.diag(Tab).round(1))

```

## Call 20

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
rc=28
best=None
for seed in range(6):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=rc,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fc@Vcc[:rc].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
oc=np.argsort(-kc); Sc,Mc,kc=Sc[:,oc],Mc[:,oc],kc[oc]
Ac=Mc.T@Vcc[:rc]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15)
Tc=(Ac/fl); Tc=Tc/np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1,keepdims=True)
Dl=W['ln1_w.3']; s=slice(64*3,64*3+64)
Q=W['W_q.3'][s]*Dl;K=W['W_k.3'][s]*Dl;V=W['W_v.3'][s]*Dl;O=W['W_o.3'][:,s]
UL,SL,_=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'],full_matrices=False)
print('OV (L3H3) sv',np.linalg.svd(O@V,compute_uv=False)[:64:3])
print('V sv',np.linalg.svd(V,compute_uv=False)[:64:3]); print('O sv',np.linalg.svd(O,compute_uv=False)[:64:3])
print('Q sv',np.linalg.svd(Q,compute_uv=False)[:64:3]); print('K sv',np.linalg.svd(K,compute_uv=False)[:64:3])
Vr=np.linalg.svd(V,full_matrices=False)[2]; Qr=np.linalg.svd(Q,full_matrices=False)[2]; Kr=np.linalg.svd(K,full_matrices=False)[2]
Ol=np.linalg.svd(O,full_matrices=False)[0]
Vraw=np.linalg.svd(W['W_v.3'][s],full_matrices=False)[2]
L=UL[:,:28]
print('O left top28 vs L cos',np.linalg.svd(Ol[:,:28].T@L,compute_uv=False).round(2))
print('V right vs L cos',np.linalg.svd(Vr[:28]@L,compute_uv=False).round(2))
print('Q right vs L',np.linalg.svd(Qr[:28]@L,compute_uv=False).round(2))
print('K right vs L',np.linalg.svd(Kr[:28]@L,compute_uv=False).round(2))
print('Q right vs K right top 40',np.linalg.svd(Qr[:40]@Kr[:40].T,compute_uv=False).round(2))
print('V right vs Q right top 28',np.linalg.svd(Vr[:28]@Qr[:40].T,compute_uv=False).round(2))
# char->logit map
Lt=(L.T@(O@(V@Tc.T)))  # 28 x rc
print('per-comp |map|',np.linalg.norm(Lt,axis=0).round(2))
print('sv map',np.linalg.svd(Lt,compute_uv=False).round(2))
print('kurt',kc.round(0))

import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
rc=28
best=None
for seed in range(6):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=rc,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fc@Vcc[:rc].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
oc=np.argsort(-kc); Sc,Mc,kc=Sc[:,oc],Mc[:,oc],kc[oc]
Ac=Mc.T@Vcc[:rc]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15)
Tc=(Ac/fl); Tc=Tc/np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1,keepdims=True)
keep=[i for i in range(384) if i not in [21,39,58,99,184,383,5,219,90,53,214,189]]
P3=W['W_proj.3']
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(P3[keep],full_matrices=False)
L=np.zeros((384,24)); L[keep]=U[:,:24]
Dl=W['ln1_w.3']; s=slice(64*3,64*3+64)
OV=W['W_o.3'][:,s]@(W['W_v.3'][s]*Dl)
lc=(L.T@(OV@Tc.T)).T   # comps x 24
ln=np.linalg.norm(lc,axis=1)
Gl=(lc@lc.T)/np.outer(ln,ln)
print('norm l_c',ln.round(2))
print('Gram of l_c'); print(Gl.round(1))
# direct bigram: neuron-weighted output of L0 MLP in L
Y=np.zeros((rc,24))
for c in range(rc):
    sel=np.where(Sc[:,c]>3)[0]
    if len(sel)==0: continue
    Y[c]=(L.T@(Pr[:,idx[sel]]@Sc[sel,c]))
Bg=Y@lc.T/ (ln[None,:]**2)
Bg=Bg-Bg.mean(1,keepdims=True)
print('bigram estimate rows=cur cols=next (centered)')
print('    '+' '.join(f'{i:5d}' for i in range(rc)))
for c in range(rc):
    print(f'{c:3d} '+' '.join(f'{v:5.1f}' for v in Bg[c]))
print('row norm of Y',np.linalg.norm(Y,axis=1).round(1))

```

## Call 24

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
rc=28
best=None
for seed in range(6):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=rc,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fc@Vcc[:rc].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
oc=np.argsort(-kc); Sc,Mc,kc=Sc[:,oc],Mc[:,oc],kc[oc]
Ac=Mc.T@Vcc[:rc]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15)
Tc=(Ac/fl)
print('t norm before unit',np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1).round(2))
Tc=Tc/np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1,keepdims=True)
Tm=Tc-Tc.mean(0,keepdims=True)
print('comps sorted by kurt',kc.round(0))
print('n>3',(Sc>3).sum(0)); print('sum z>3 per comp',np.where(Sc>3,Sc,0).sum(0).round(0))
for j in range(6):
    s=slice(64*j,64*j+64)
    for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
        M=W[f'{nm}.0'][s]*D1
        u,sv,vt=np.linalg.svd(M@Tm.T,full_matrices=False)
        print(f'H{j} {nm} per-comp norm',np.linalg.norm(M@Tm.T,axis=0).round(2),' sv',sv[:6].round(2))
    for nm in ['W_v','W_k','W_q']:
        M=W[f'{nm}.0'][s]*D1
        u,sv,vt=np.linalg.svd(M@Tm.T,full_matrices=False)
        print(f'   {nm} sv0 comps',(vt[0]).round(2)); print(f'   {nm} sv1 comps',(vt[1]).round(2))

```

## Call 25

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Fp=F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
rc=28
best=None
for seed in range(6):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=rc,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fc@Vcc[:rc].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
oc=np.argsort(-kc); Sc,Mc,kc=Sc[:,oc],Mc[:,oc],kc[oc]
Ac=Mc.T@Vcc[:rc]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15); Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
# confirm the identity of the special comps in this run
H3V=np.linalg.norm((W['W_v.0'][192:256]*D1)@(Tu-Tu.mean(0)).T,axis=0)
print('t norm',tn.round(2)); print('H3 V norm',H3V.round(2))
vow=np.argsort(H3V)[:5]; print('low-V comps',vow, 'tn',tn[vow].round(2))
rest=[c for c in range(rc) if c not in vow]
# Output of L0 neurons into logit subspace
keep=[i for i in range(384) if i not in [21,39,58,99,184,383,5,219,90,53,214,189]]
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'][keep],full_matrices=False)
L=np.zeros((384,27)); L[keep]=U[:,:27]
Ps=Pr[:,idx]
Y=np.zeros((rc,27)); Yn=np.zeros(rc)
for c in range(rc):
    sel=np.where(Sc[:,c]>2.5)[0]
    Y[c]=L.T@(Ps[:,sel]@Sc[sel,c])
    Yn[c]=len(sel)
Ym=Y-Y.mean(0)
print('row norms',np.linalg.norm(Y,axis=1).round(1))
sa=np.linalg.svd(Ym,compute_uv=False); print('sv all',sa.round(1))
for name,grp in [('vowels',list(vow)),('others',rest)]:
    M=Y[grp]-Y[grp].mean(0); s=np.linalg.svd(M,compute_uv=False)
    print(name,'sv',s.round(1),'top5 energy %.2f'%((s[:5]**2).sum()/(s**2).sum()))
Uv=np.linalg.svd(Y[list(vow)],full_matrices=False)[2][:4]
Ur=np.linalg.svd(Y[rest],full_matrices=False)[2][:8]
print('cos vowelrows vs other rows',np.linalg.svd(Uv@Ur.T,compute_uv=False).round(2))
# bigram share per cur comp
frac=np.linalg.norm(Fp,axis=1)**2/np.linalg.norm(F,axis=1)**2
for c in range(rc):
    sel=np.where(Sc[:,c]>3)[0]
    print(c,'V' if c in vow else ' ','n',len(sel),'prev-share mean %.2f'%frac[sel].mean() if len(sel) else '')

```

## Call 26

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Os=W['W_o.0'][:,64*3:64*3+64]
Uo,So,_=np.linalg.svd(Os,full_matrices=False)
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*Uo[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
print('Scc',Scc[:44].round(2))
runs=[]
for r in [26,28,30,32,34,36,40]:
    for seed in range(3):
        ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=2500,tol=2e-5,random_state=seed)
        Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fc@Vcc[:r].T)
        kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
        A=(ica.mixing_.T@Vcc[:r]); A=A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1,keepdims=True)
        runs.append((r,A,kur))
ref=[x for x in runs if x[0]==30][0]
Aref=ref[1]; kref=ref[2]
hit=np.zeros((len(Aref),len(runs)))
for i,(r,A,k) in enumerate(runs):
    C=np.abs(Aref@A.T).max(1)
    hit[:,i]=C
o=np.argsort(-kref)
print('r list',[x[0] for x in runs])
print('ref kurt',kref[o].round(0))
print('median cos with other runs',np.median(hit[o],axis=1).round(2))
print('min cos',hit[o].min(1).round(2))
# how the top axes correspond across r=26 / r=40
for rr in [26,40]:
    x=[y for y in runs if y[0]==rr][0]
    print('r',rr,'n with cos>.9 to ref',(np.abs(Aref@x[1].T).max(1)>.9).sum(),'n>.8',(np.abs(Aref@x[1].T).max(1)>.8).sum())

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Oh=[W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64] for j in range(6)]
Vh=[W['W_v.0'][64*j:64*j+64]*D1 for j in range(6)]
U3=np.linalg.svd(Oh[3],full_matrices=False)[0]
B2,_=np.linalg.qr((D2[:,None]*U3[:,:28]))
Fc=F-F@B2@B2.T
Ucc,Scc,Vcc=np.linalg.svd(Fc,full_matrices=False)
r=32
best=None
for seed in range(6):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fc@Vcc[:r].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
oc=np.argsort(-kc); Sc,Mc,kc=Sc[:,oc],Mc[:,oc],kc[oc]
Ac=Mc.T@Vcc[:r]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15); Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
# component in functional coordinates (D2-weighted)
Aw=Ac*D2; Aw=Aw/np.linalg.norm(Aw,axis=1,keepdims=True)
print('kurt',kc.round(0))
print('tnorm',tn.round(2))
print('n>3',(Sc>3).sum(0))
for j in [0,1,2,4,5]:
    Uh=np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0]
    print(f'frac in O_H{j} top16',np.linalg.norm(Aw@Uh[:,:16],axis=1).round(2))
UV=np.linalg.svd(np.vstack(Vh),full_matrices=False)[2]
print('frac in V-stack top40',np.linalg.norm(Tu@UV[:40].T,axis=1).round(2))
UQ=np.linalg.svd(np.vstack([W['W_q.0']*D1,W['W_k.0']*D1]),full_matrices=False)[2]
print('frac in QK-stack top40',np.linalg.norm(Tu@UQ[:40].T,axis=1).round(2))
print('H3 V norm',np.linalg.norm(Vh[3]@(Tu-Tu.mean(0)).T,axis=0).round(2))
print('H3 K norm',np.linalg.norm((W['W_k.0'][192:256]*D1)@(Tu-Tu.mean(0)).T,axis=0).round(2))
print('L3H3 V norm',np.linalg.norm((W['W_v.3'][192:256]*W['ln1_w.3'])@(Tu-Tu.mean(0)).T,axis=0).round(2))

```

print('O sv per head (idx 0,4,8,12,16,20,28,40)')
for j in range(6):
    s=np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],compute_uv=False); print(j,s[[0,4,8,12,16,20,28,40]])
stack=[]
for j,k in [(3,28),(5,24),(0,24),(1,16),(2,16),(4,16)]:
    stack.append(np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:k])
S=np.hstack(stack)
for name,mat in [('H3 only',stack[0]),('H3+H5',np.hstack(stack[:2])),('H3+H5+H0',np.hstack(stack[:3])),('all',S)]:
    Bq,_=np.linalg.qr(D2[:,None]*mat)
    Fr=F-F@Bq@Bq.T
    s=np.linalg.svd(Fr,compute_uv=False)
    print(name,'rank basis',Bq.shape[1],'rest sv',s[:36:1].round(2))

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=220)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Oh=[W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64] for j in range(6)]
stack=[]
for j,k in [(3,28),(5,24),(0,24),(1,16),(2,16),(4,16)]:
    stack.append(np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:k])
Bq,_=np.linalg.qr(D2[:,None]*np.hstack(stack))
Fr=F-F@Bq@Bq.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Fr,full_matrices=False)
res={}
for r in [22,24,26,28,30,32,36]:
    best=None
    for seed in range(4):
        ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=2500,tol=2e-5,random_state=seed)
        Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fr@Vt[:r].T)
        kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
        if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
    _,kc,Sc,Mc=best
    sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
    o=np.argsort(-kc); kc=kc[o]; Sc=Sc[:,o]; Mc=Mc[:,o]
    A=Mc.T@Vt[:r]; A=A/np.linalg.norm(A,axis=1,keepdims=True)
    res[r]=(kc,Sc,A)
    print('r',r,'kurt',kc.round(0)); print('     n>3',(Sc>3).sum(0))
Aref=res[30][2]
for r in [22,24,26,28,32,36]:
    c=np.abs(Aref@res[r][2].T).max(1)
    print('match to r30 comps from r',r,c.round(2))

## Call 30

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Oh=[W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64] for j in range(6)]
stack=[]
for j,k in [(3,28),(5,24),(0,24),(1,16),(2,16),(4,16)]:
    stack.append(np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:k])
Bq,_=np.linalg.qr(D2[:,None]*np.hstack(stack))
Fr=F-F@Bq@Bq.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Fr,full_matrices=False)
r=26
best=None
for seed in range(8):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fr@Vt[:r].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
o=np.argsort(-kc); kc=kc[o]; Sc=Sc[:,o]; Mc=Mc[:,o]
Ac=Mc.T@Vt[:r]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15); Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
Tm=Tu-Tu.mean(0)
print('kurt',kc.round(0)); print('n>3',(Sc>3).sum(0)); print('tn',tn.round(2))
for h in range(6):
    s=slice(64*h,64*h+64)
    print(f'H{h} V',np.linalg.norm((W['W_v.0'][s]*D1)@Tm.T,axis=0).round(2))
    print(f'H{h} K',np.linalg.norm((W['W_k.0'][s]*D1)@Tm.T,axis=0).round(2))
print('L3H3 V',np.linalg.norm((W['W_v.3'][192:256]*W['ln1_w.3'])@Tm.T,axis=0).round(2))
# prev vectors
V3=W['W_v.0'][192:256]*D1
wp=((Oh[3]@(V3@Tm.T)).T)*D2   # k x 384 raw row coords
print('|wp|',np.linalg.norm(wp,axis=1).round(2))
Prv=F@wp.T
Prv=(Prv-Prv.mean(0))/Prv.std(0)
B=np.zeros((r,r))
for c in range(r):
    sel=np.where(Sc[:,c]>2.5)[0]
    w=Sc[sel,c]
    B[c]=(Prv[sel]*w[:,None]).sum(0)
print('table rows cur / cols prev : sum of z(prev-sensitivity) weighted by cur loading')
print('    '+' '.join(f'{i:5d}' for i in range(r)))
for c in range(r):
    print(f'{c:3d} '+' '.join(f'{v:5.0f}' for v in B[c]))
Bo=B-np.diag(np.diag(B))
Bo=Bo-Bo.mean(0,keepdims=True)-Bo.mean(1,keepdims=True)+Bo.mean()
Sy=(Bo+Bo.T)/2
ev,evec=np.linalg.eigh(Sy)
print('eig',ev.round(0))
print('lowest vec',evec[:,0].round(2)); print('2nd low',evec[:,1].round(2)); print('highest',evec[:,-1].round(2))

```

    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
o=np.argsort(-kc); kc=kc[o]; Sc=Sc[:,o]; Mc=Mc[:,o]
Ac=Mc.T@Vt[:r]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15); Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
Tu=Tu[:23]; Tm=Tu-Tu.mean(0)
print('comp idx ',np.arange(23))
for l,hs in [(0,range(6)),(1,[0,2]),(2,[1]),(3,[0,3,4])]:
    Dl=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']
    for h in hs:
        s=slice(64*h,64*h+64)
        for nm in ['W_q','W_k','W_v']:
            M=(W[f'{nm}.{l}'][s]*Dl)@Tm.T
            u,sv,vt=np.linalg.svd(M,full_matrices=False)
            print(f'L{l}H{h} {nm} sv',sv[:4].round(2)); print('    v0',vt[0].round(2)); print('    v1',vt[1].round(2))
# neuron cofactor: correlation between comps in loadings (shared neurons), and negative loads
Z=Sc[:,:23]
C=np.corrcoef(Z.T)
ev,evec=np.linalg.eigh(C)
print('corr eig',ev.round(2))
print('top vec',evec[:,-1].round(2)); print('2nd',evec[:,-2].round(2)); print('low',evec[:,0].round(2))

```

## Call 32

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Oh=[W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64] for j in range(6)]
stack=[]
for j,k in [(3,28),(5,24),(0,24),(1,16),(2,16),(4,16)]:
    stack.append(np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:k])
Bq,_=np.linalg.qr(D2[:,None]*np.hstack(stack))
Fr=F-F@Bq@Bq.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Fr,full_matrices=False)
r=26
best=None
for seed in range(8):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fr@Vt[:r].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
o=np.argsort(-kc); kc=kc[o]; Sc=Sc[:,o]; Mc=Mc[:,o]
Ac=Mc.T@Vt[:r]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15); Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
Tu=Tu[:23]; Tm=Tu-Tu.mean(0)
Sc23=Sc[:,:23]
keep=[i for i in range(384) if i not in [21,39,58,99,184,383,5,219,90,53,214,189]]
Ub,Sb,_=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'][keep],full_matrices=False)
L=np.zeros((384,24)); L[keep]=Ub[:,:24]
y0=(L.T@Tm.T).T
n0=np.linalg.norm(y0,axis=1)
print('direct |L t_c|',n0.round(2))
G0=(y0@y0.T)/np.outer(n0,n0)
print('cos direct'); 
print('    '+' '.join(f'{i:4d}' for i in range(23)))
for c in range(23):
    print(f'{c:3d} '+' '.join(f'{v:4.1f}' for v in G0[c]))
ev,evec=np.linalg.eigh(G0)
print('eig',ev[::-1].round(2))
print('v1',evec[:,-1].round(2)); print('v2',evec[:,-2].round(2)); print('v3',evec[:,-3].round(2))
# Y from L0 MLP
Ps=Pr[:,idx]
Y=np.zeros((23,24))
for c in range(23):
    sel=np.where(Sc23[:,c]>2.5)[0]
    Y[c]=L.T@(Ps[:,sel]@Sc23[sel,c])
Ym=Y-Y.mean(0); ny=np.linalg.norm(Ym,axis=1)
Gy=(Ym@Ym.T)/np.outer(ny,ny)
ev2,evec2=np.linalg.eigh(Gy)
print('eig Y',ev2[::-1].round(2)); print('v1',evec2[:,-1].round(2)); print('v2',evec2[:,-2].round(2))
print('corr of direct and Y cos pattern',np.corrcoef(G0[np.triu_indices(23,1)],Gy[np.triu_indices(23,1)])[0,1].round(2))

## Call 33

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Oh=[W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64] for j in range(6)]
stack=[]
for j,k in [(3,28),(5,24),(0,24),(1,16),(2,16),(4,16)]:
    stack.append(np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:k])
Bq,_=np.linalg.qr(D2[:,None]*np.hstack(stack))
Fr=F-F@Bq@Bq.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Fr,full_matrices=False)
r=26
best=None
for seed in range(8):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fr@Vt[:r].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
o=np.argsort(-kc); kc=kc[o]; Sc=Sc[:,o]; Mc=Mc[:,o]
Ac=Mc.T@Vt[:r]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15); Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
Tu=Tu[:23]; Tm=Tu-Tu.mean(0)
Sc23=Sc[:,:23]
keep=[i for i in range(384) if i not in [21,39,58,99,184,383,5,219,90,53,214,189]]
Ub,Sb,_=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'][keep],full_matrices=False)
L=np.zeros((384,24)); L[keep]=Ub[:,:24]
Dl=W['ln1_w.3']; s=slice(192,256)
OV=W['W_o.3'][:,s]@(W['W_v.3'][s]*Dl)
lc=(L.T@(OV@Tm.T)).T
ln=np.linalg.norm(lc,axis=1)
Gl=(lc@lc.T)/np.outer(ln,ln)
print('|l_c|',ln.round(2))
print('cos(l_c,l_c) offdiag: max %.2f, mean abs %.2f'%(np.abs(Gl-np.eye(23)).max(),np.abs(Gl-np.eye(23)).mean()))
print('sv of l',np.linalg.svd(lc,compute_uv=False).round(2))
# dual axes (pseudo-inverse): logit axis functional
pin=np.linalg.pinv(lc)     # 24 x 23 ; lc @ pin = I
# Estimate bigram from direct path
y0=(L.T@Tm.T).T
Bd=y0@pin
# from L0 MLP
Ps=Pr[:,idx]
Y=np.zeros((23,24))
for c in range(23):
    sel=np.where(Sc23[:,c]>2.5)[0]
    Y[c]=L.T@(Ps[:,sel]@Sc23[sel,c])
Bm=Y@pin
def show(B,name):
    B=B-B.mean(1,keepdims=True); B=B/B.std()
    print(name)
    print('    '+' '.join(f'{i:4d}' for i in range(23)))
    for c in range(23):
        print(f'{c:3d} '+' '.join(f'{v:4.1f}' for v in B[c]))
    print('col mean',B.mean(0).round(1))
    print('col std',B.std(0).round(1))
show(Bd,'direct bigram (next col given cur row)')
show(Bm,'MLP bigram')
print('corr Bd Bm',np.corrcoef(Bd.ravel(),Bm.ravel())[0,1].round(2))

```

```python
import numpy as np, warnings, re
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Oh=[W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64] for j in range(6)]
stack=[]
for j,k in [(3,28),(5,24),(0,24),(1,16),(2,16),(4,16)]:
    stack.append(np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:k])
Bq,_=np.linalg.qr(D2[:,None]*np.hstack(stack))
Fr=F-F@Bq@Bq.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Fr,full_matrices=False)
r=26
best=None
for seed in range(8):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fr@Vt[:r].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
o=np.argsort(-kc); kc=kc[o]; Sc=Sc[:,o]; Mc=Mc[:,o]
Ac=Mc.T@Vt[:r]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15); Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
Tu=Tu[:23]; Tm=Tu-Tu.mean(0); Sc23=Sc[:,:23]
keep=[i for i in range(384) if i not in [21,39,58,99,184,383,5,219,90,53,214,189]]
Ub,Sb,_=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'][keep],full_matrices=False)
L=np.zeros((384,24)); L[keep]=Ub[:,:24]
Dl=W['ln1_w.3']; s=slice(192,256)
OV=W['W_o.3'][:,s]@(W['W_v.3'][s]*Dl)
lc=(L.T@(OV@Tm.T)).T
ln=np.linalg.norm(lc,axis=1); lu=lc/ln[:,None]
pin=np.linalg.pinv(lc)
def tab(Y,mode):
    B=(Y@pin) if mode=='pinv' else (Y@lu.T)
    B=B-B.mean(1,keepdims=True); return B
Ys={}
Ys['direct']=(L.T@Tm.T).T
Ps=Pr[:,idx]
Ym=np.zeros((23,24))
for c in range(23):
    sel=np.where(Sc23[:,c]>2.5)[0]
    Ym[c]=L.T@(Ps[:,sel]@Sc23[sel,c])
Ys['mlp0']=Ym
for l in [0,1,2,3]:
    fc=W[f'W_fc.{l}']; pj=W[f'W_proj.{l}']; dl=W[f'ln2_w.{l}']
    Ys[f'lin{l}']=(L.T@(pj@(fc@(dl[:,None]*Tm.T)))).T
names=list(Ys)
for mode in ['pinv','cos']:
    T=[tab(Ys[n],mode).ravel() for n in names]
    print(mode,names); print(np.corrcoef(T).round(2))
print({n:np.linalg.norm(Ys[n],axis=1).mean().round(2) for n in names})

import numpy as np, warnings, re
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
from collections import Counter
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=250)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Oh=[W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64] for j in range(6)]
stack=[]
for j,k in [(3,28),(5,24),(0,24),(1,16),(2,16),(4,16)]:
    stack.append(np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:k])
Bq,_=np.linalg.qr(D2[:,None]*np.hstack(stack))
Fr=F-F@Bq@Bq.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Fr,full_matrices=False)
r=26
best=None
for seed in range(8):
    ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=3000,tol=1e-5,random_state=seed)
    Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fr@Vt[:r].T)
    kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
    if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
_,kc,Sc,Mc=best
sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
o=np.argsort(-kc); kc=kc[o]; Sc=Sc[:,o]; Mc=Mc[:,o]
Ac=Mc.T@Vt[:r]
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15); Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
Tu=Tu[:23]; Tm=Tu-Tu.mean(0); Sc23=Sc[:,:23]
keep=[i for i in range(384) if i not in [21,39,58,99,184,383,5,219,90,53,214,189]]
Ub,Sb,_=np.linalg.svd(W['W_proj.3'][keep],full_matrices=False)
L=np.zeros((384,24)); L[keep]=Ub[:,:24]
Dl=W['ln1_w.3']; s=slice(192,256)
OV=W['W_o.3'][:,s]@(W['W_v.3'][s]*Dl)
lc=(L.T@(OV@Tm.T)).T
pin=np.linalg.pinv(lc)
Ys={'direct':(L.T@Tm.T).T}
Ps=Pr[:,idx]
Ym=np.zeros((23,24))
for c in range(23):
    sel=np.where(Sc23[:,c]>2.5)[0]

Ys['mlp0']=Ym
for l in [1,2,3]:

    Ys[f'lin{l}']=(L.T@(pj@(fc@(dl[:,None]*Tm.T)))).T
M=np.zeros((23,23))
for n,Y in Ys.items():

M=M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True)

py="""import os
import sys
from collections import defaultdict

class Config(object):
    def __init__(self, name, value=None):
        self.name = name
        self.value = value
        self.items = []

    def get(self, key, default=None):
        if key in self.items:
            return self.items[key]
        return default

    def set(self, key, value):
        self.items[key] = value

    def to_dict(self):
        result = {}
        for key, value in self.items.items():
            if isinstance(value, list):
                result[key] = [item.to_dict() for item in value]
            else:
                result[key] = value
        return result

def load_file(path, encoding='utf-8'):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        raise ValueError('file not found: %s' % path)
    with open(path, 'r', encoding=encoding) as f:
        data = f.read()
    return data

def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--input', type=str, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=str, default=None)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    return args

class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_size, hidden_size, num_layers=1):
        super(Model, self).__init__()
        self.input_size = input_size
        self.hidden_size = hidden_size
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
        for i in range(num_layers):
            self.layers.append(nn.Linear(input_size, hidden_size))

    def forward(self, x):
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x)
        return x

class TestCase(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.client = Client()
        self.request = self.client.get('/test/')

    def test_response(self):
        response = self.client.post('/api/', data={'name': 'test'})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertTrue(response.content)

    def test_list(self):
        result = list(map(str, range(10)))
        self.assertEqual(len(result), 10)

def main():
    args = parse_args()
    logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    logger.info('starting')
    try:
        data = load_file(args.input)
    except IOError as e:
        logger.error(str(e))
        sys.exit(1)
    if args.output is not None:
        with open(args.output, 'w') as f:
            f.write(data)
    return 0

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
"""
def norm(t):
    out=[]
    for line in t.lower().split('\n'):
        lead=len(line)-len(line.lstrip(' '))
        body=re.sub(r'[^a-z]+',' ',line[lead:]).strip()
        body=re.sub(r' +',' ',body)
        if body: out.append(' '*lead+body)
    return '\n'.join(out)+'\n'
def ref(txt,n=23):
    t=norm(txt); cnt=Counter(t); ch=[c for c,_ in cnt.most_common(n)]
    print(repr(''.join(ch)),len(t))
    i={c:k for k,c in enumerate(ch)}
    B=np.ones((len(ch),len(ch)))*0.5
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]):
        if a in i and b in i: B[i[a],i[b]]+=1
    P=B/B.sum(1,keepdims=True)
    R=np.log(P); R=R-R.mean(1,keepdims=True)
    return ch,R
def search(M,R,restarts=200,seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=M.shape[0]
    mask=~np.eye(n,dtype=bool)
    Mz=(M-M[mask].mean())/M[mask].std()
    Rz=(R-R[mask].mean())/R[mask].std()
    bestv=-9;bestp=None
    for rs in range(restarts):
        p=rng.permutation(n)
        cur=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum()
        improved=True
        while improved:
            improved=False
            for a in range(n):
                for b in range(a+1,n):
                    p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
                    v=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9:
                        cur=v; improved=True
                    else:
                        p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
        if cur>bestv: bestv=cur;bestp=p.copy()
    return bestv/mask.sum(),bestp
rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
for name,t in [('english',eng),('romaji',rom),('python',py)]:
    ch,R=ref(t)
    v,p=search(M,R,restarts=40)
    nulls=[]
    for k in range(4):
        G=rng.normal(size=M.shape); G=G-G.mean(1,keepdims=True)
        nulls.append(search(G,R,restarts=10,seed=k)[0])
    print(name,'best corr %.3f'%v,'null',np.round(nulls,3))
    print('   mapping comp->char',''.join(ch[i] if ch[i] not in ' \n' else {' ':'_','\n':'$'}[ch[i]] for i in p))

mugon de atama wo sageta"""
def ref(t,ch):
    i={c:k for k,c in enumerate(ch)}
    B=np.ones((len(ch),len(ch)))*0.5
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]):
        if a in i and b in i: B[i[a],i[b]]+=1
    P=B/B.sum(1,keepdims=True)
    R=np.log(P); return R-R.mean(1,keepdims=True)
def search(M,R,restarts,seed=0,fix=None):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); n=M.shape[0]
    mask=~np.eye(n,dtype=bool)
    Mz=(M-M[mask].mean())/M[mask].std(); Rz=(R-R[mask].mean())/R[mask].std()
    bestv=-9;bestp=None;vals=[]
    free=[i for i in range(n) if fix is None or i!=fix[0]]
    for rs in range(restarts):
        p=rng.permutation(n)
        if fix is not None:
            j=int(np.where(p==fix[1])[0][0]); p[[j,fix[0]]]=p[[fix[0],j]]
        cur=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum(); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for ia,a in enumerate(free):
                for b in free[ia+1:]:
                    p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
                    v=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9: cur=v;imp=True
                    else: p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
        vals.append(cur/mask.sum())
        if cur>bestv: bestv=cur;bestp=p.copy()
    return bestv/mask.sum(),bestp,np.array(vals)
def fix_ch(ch): return ''.join({' ':'_','\n':'$'}.get(c,c) for c in ch)
chE=list(' etanroishldcumfpgwybvk'); chR=list(' aoiunktesrhmwdgyzbjcfp')
RE=ref(eng,chE); RR=ref(rom,chR)
rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
for name,ch,R in [('english',chE,RE),('romaji',chR,RR)]:
    for fix in [None,(7,0)]:
        v,p,vals=search(M,R,restarts=60,fix=fix)
        print(name,'fix',fix,'best %.3f'%v,'top vals',np.sort(vals)[-5:].round(3))
        mp=[ch[i] for i in p]
        print('   ',fix_ch(mp))
        print('    top next by label:',' '.join(f"{'_' if mp[c]==' ' else mp[c]}:{''.join('_' if mp[x]==' ' else mp[x] for x in np.argsort(-M[c])[:4])}" for c in range(23)))
    nulls=[search((G:=rng.normal(size=M.shape))-G.mean(1,keepdims=True),R,restarts=30,seed=k)[0] for k in range(3)]
    print('   null(30 restarts)',np.round(nulls,3))
    print('t',time.time()-t0)

the province before joining the main stream near the border"""
rom="""wagahai wa neko de aru namae wa mada nai dokode umareta ka tonto kentou ga tsukanu nandemo usugurai jimejime shita tokoro de nyaa nyaa naite ita koto dake wa kioku shite iru wagahai wa koko de hajimete ningen to iu mono wo mita shikamo ato de kiku to sore wa shosei to iu ningen chuu de ichiban douaku na shuzoku de atta sou da kono shosei to iu no wa tokidoki wareware wo tsukamaete nite kuu to iu hanashi de aru shikashi sono touji wa nani to iu kangae mo nakatta kara betsudan osoroshii to mo omowanakatta tada kare no tanagokoro ni nosete suu to mochiagerareta toki nandaka fuwafuwa shita kanji ga atta bakari de aru aru hi no kuregata no koto de aru hitori no genin ga rashoumon no shita de amayami wo matte ita hiroi mon no shita ni wa kono otoko no hoka ni dare mo inai tada tokorodokoro niri no hageta ookina maruhashira ni kirigirisu ga ippiki tomatte iru rashoumon ga suzakuooji ni aru ijou wa kono otoko no hoka ni mo ameyami wo suru ichimegasa ya momieboshi ga mou nisannin wa arisou na mono de aru sore ga kono otoko no hoka ni wa dare mo inai watakushi wa sono hito wo tsune ni sensei to yonde ita dakara koko de mo tada sensei to kaku dake de honmyou wa uchiakenai kore wa seken wo habakaru enryo to iu yori mo sono hou ga watakushi ni totte shizen dakara de aru watakushi wa sono hito no kioku wo yobiokosu goto ni sugu sensei to iitaku naru fude wo tottemo kokoromochi wa onaji koto de aru kunizakai no nagai tonneru wo nukeru to yukiguni de atta yoru no soko ga shiroku natta shingoujo ni kisha ga tomatta mukaigawa no zaseki kara musume ga tatte kite shimamura no mae no garasumado wo otoshita yuki no reiki ga nagarekonda musume wa mado ippai ni nori dashite tooku e sakebu you ni eki no hito ni koe wo kaketa kare wa sore wo kiite mimi wo sumashita ano toki no koto wo omoidasu to ima de mo mune ga itaku naru haha wa daidokoro de asagohan no shitaku wo shinagara chiisana koe de uta wo utatte ita chichi wa mainichi hayaku kara kaisha e dete itta hito no kokoro to iu mono wa makoto ni fushigi na mono de aru toshi wo totta rouba ga hitori shizuka ni suwatte ita omae wa doko kara kita no ka to kiku to otoko wa nani mo kotaezu ni tada mugon de atama wo sageta"""
def ref(t,ch):
    i={c:k for k,c in enumerate(ch)}
    B=np.ones((len(ch),len(ch)))*0.3
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]):
        if a in i and b in i: B[i[a],i[b]]+=1
    P=B/B.sum(1,keepdims=True)
    return np.log(P)
def search(M,Rlog,restarts,seed=0,fix=None):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); n=M.shape[0]
    mask=~np.eye(n,dtype=bool)
    Mz=(M-M[mask].mean())/M[mask].std(); Rz0=dc(Rlog); Rz=(Rz0-Rz0[mask].mean())/Rz0[mask].std()
    bestv=-9;bestp=None;vals=[]
    free=[i for i in range(n) if fix is None or i!=fix[0]]
    for rs in range(restarts):
        p=rng.permutation(n)
        if fix is not None:
            j=int(np.where(p==fix[1])[0][0]); p[[j,fix[0]]]=p[[fix[0],j]]
        cur=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum(); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for ia,a in enumerate(free):
                for b in free[ia+1:]:
                    p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
                    v=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9: cur=v;imp=True
                    else: p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
        vals.append(cur/mask.sum())
        if cur>bestv: bestv=cur;bestp=p.copy()
    return bestv/mask.sum(),bestp,np.array(vals)
chE=list(' etanroishldcumfpgwybvk'); chR=list(' aoiunktesrhmwdgyzbjcfp')
RE=ref(eng,chE); RR=ref(rom,chR)
rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
for name,ch,R in [('english',chE,RE),('romaji',chR,RR)]:
    v,p,vals=search(Md,R,restarts=80)
    mp=[ch[i] for i in p]
    print(name,'best %.3f'%v,'top',np.sort(vals)[-6:].round(3))
    print('   comp->char',''.join('_' if c==' ' else c for c in mp))
    print('    next:',' '.join(f"{'_' if mp[c]==' ' else mp[c]}:{''.join('_' if mp[x]==' ' else mp[x] for x in np.argsort(-Md[c])[:5])}" for c in range(23)))
    nulls=[]
    for k in range(6):
        G=rng.normal(size=Md.shape); G=dc(G)
        nulls.append(search(G,R,restarts=30,seed=k)[0])
    print('   null',np.round(nulls,3))
    print(time.time()-t0)

for l in [1,2,3]:

def dc(M):
    M=M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True); M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True); M=M/M.std(0,keepdims=True); M=M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True); return M/M.std(1,keepdims=True)
for n,Y in Ys.items():

def gram(A):

iu=np.triu_indices(23,1)
print('corr gram(l) vs gram(Y)',np.corrcoef(gram(lc)[iu],gram(Yall)[iu])[0,1].round(3))
print('corr gram(l) vs gram(direct)',np.corrcoef(gram(lc)[iu],gram(Ys['direct'])[iu])[0,1].round(3))

    Y=(L.T@(pj@(fc@(dl[:,None]*Tm.T)))).T
    T.append(dc(Y@pin))
print('corr between lin tables',np.corrcoef([t.ravel() for t in T]).round(2))
Ml=dc(sum(T))
Sy=(Ml+Ml.T)/2
e,v=np.linalg.eigh(Sy)
print('sym eig',e.round(1))
print('lowest evec',v[:,0].round(2)); print('order',np.argsort(v[:,0]))
print('2nd lowest',v[:,1].round(2)); print('order',np.argsort(v[:,1]))
print('highest',v[:,-1].round(2)); print('order',np.argsort(v[:,-1]))
print('rows top next')
for c in range(23):
    o=np.argsort(-Ml[c]); print(c,o[:7],Ml[c][o[:7]].round(1),'| low',o[-5:], 'self %.1f'%Ml[c,c])
ev,evec=np.linalg.eigh(np.corrcoef(Ml)); print('row eig',ev[::-1][:7].round(2))
print(' r1',evec[:,-1].round(2)); print(' r2',evec[:,-2].round(2))
ev2,evec2=np.linalg.eigh(np.corrcoef(Ml.T)); print('col eig',ev2[::-1][:7].round(2))
print(' c1',evec2[:,-1].round(2)); print(' c2',evec2[:,-2].round(2))
print(repr(np.round(Ml,1).tolist()).replace(' ',''))

```

py=""" import os import sys from collections import defaultdict
 class config object
     def init self name value none
         self name name
         self value value
         self items
     def get self key default none
         if key in self items
             return self items key
         return default
     def set self key value
         self items key value
     def to dict self
         result
         for key value in self items items
             if isinstance value list
                 result key item to dict for item in value
             else
                 result key value
         return result
 def load file path encoding utf
     if not os path exists path
         raise valueerror file not found s path
     with open path r encoding encoding as f
         data f read
     return data
 def parse args argv none
     parser argparse argumentparser
     parser add argument input type str required true
     parser add argument output type str default none
     args parser parse args argv
     return args
 class model nn module
     def init self input size hidden size num layers
         super model self init
         self input size input size
         self hidden size hidden size
         self layers nn modulelist
         for i in range num layers
             self layers append nn linear input size hidden size
     def forward self x
         for layer in self layers
             x layer x
         return x
 class testcase unittest testcase
     def setup self
         self client client
         self request self client get test
     def test response self
         response self client post api data name test
         self assertequal response status code
         self asserttrue response content
     def test list self
         result list map str range
         self assertequal len result
 def main
     args parse args
     logger logging getlogger name
     logger info starting
     try
         data load file args input
     except ioerror as e
         logger error str e
         sys exit
     if args output is not none
         with open args output w as f
             f write data
     return
 if name main
     main
"""
def dc(M):
    M=M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True); M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True); M=M/M.std(0,keepdims=True); M=M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True); return M/M.std(1,keepdims=True)
def ref(t,ch,eps=0.3):
    i={c:k for k,c in enumerate(ch)}
    B=np.ones((len(ch),len(ch)))*eps
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]):
        if a in i and b in i: B[i[a],i[b]]+=1
    P=B/B.sum(1,keepdims=True)
    return np.log(P)
print('model row eig',np.linalg.eigvalsh(np.corrcoef(Ml))[::-1][:6].round(2),'col',np.linalg.eigvalsh(np.corrcoef(Ml.T))[::-1][:6].round(2),'sym low',np.linalg.eigvalsh((Ml+Ml.T)/2)[:4].round(1))
chs={'english':list(' etanroishldcumfpgwybvk'),'romaji':list(' aoiunktesrhmwdgyzbjcfp'),'python':list(' \nesatrnilofdpugmcyvhkx')}
txts={'english':eng,'romaji':rom,'python':py}
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
for nm in chs:
    t=txts[nm]; ch=chs[nm]; n=len(t)
    for f in [.25,.5,1]:
        ee=[];ec=[];sl=[]
        for r_ in range(5 if f<1 else 1):
            st=rng.integers(0,n-int(f*n)+1); s=t[st:st+int(f*n)]
            R=dc(ref(s,ch))
            ee.append(np.linalg.eigvalsh(np.corrcoef(R))[::-1][:4]); sl.append(np.linalg.eigvalsh((R+R.T)/2)[:3])
        print(nm,'f',f,'row eig',np.mean(ee,0).round(2),'sym low',np.mean(sl,0).round(1))
def search(M,Rlog,restarts,seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); n=M.shape[0]
    mask=~np.eye(n,dtype=bool)
    Mz=(M-M[mask].mean())/M[mask].std(); Rz0=dc(Rlog); Rz=(Rz0-Rz0[mask].mean())/Rz0[mask].std()
    bestv=-9;bestp=None;vals=[]
    for rs in range(restarts):
        p=rng.permutation(n); cur=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum(); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for a in range(n):
                for b in range(a+1,n):
                    p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
                    v=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9: cur=v;imp=True
                    else: p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
        vals.append(cur/mask.sum())
        if cur>bestv: bestv=cur;bestp=p.copy()
    return bestv/mask.sum(),bestp,np.array(vals)
for nm in chs:
    ch=chs[nm]; R=ref(txts[nm],ch)
    v,p,vals=search(Ml,R,120)
    mp=[ch[i] for i in p]
    lab=lambda c:{' ':'_','\n':'$'}.get(c,c)
    print(nm,'best %.3f'%v,'top',np.sort(vals)[-5:].round(3))
    print('   comp->char',''.join(lab(c) for c in mp))
    print('   next:',' '.join(f"{lab(mp[c])}:{''.join(lab(mp[x]) for x in np.argsort(-Ml[c])[:5])}" for c in range(23)))
    print('   t',round(time.time()-t0,1))

def ref(t,ch,eps=0.3):
    i={c:k for k,c in enumerate(ch)}
    B=np.ones((len(ch),len(ch)))*eps
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]):
        if a in i and b in i: B[i[a],i[b]]+=1
    P=B/B.sum(1,keepdims=True)
    return np.log(P)
def search(M,Rlog,restarts,seed=0,fix=None):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed); n=M.shape[0]
    mask=~np.eye(n,dtype=bool)
    Mz=(M-M[mask].mean())/M[mask].std(); Rz0=dc(Rlog); Rz=(Rz0-Rz0[mask].mean())/Rz0[mask].std()
    bestv=-9;bestp=None
    free=[i for i in range(n) if fix is None or i!=fix[0]]
    for rs in range(restarts):
        p=rng.permutation(n)
        if fix is not None:
            j=int(np.where(p==fix[1])[0][0]); p[[j,fix[0]]]=p[[fix[0],j]]
        cur=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum(); imp=True
        while imp:
            imp=False
            for ia,a in enumerate(free):
                for b in free[ia+1:]:
                    p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
                    v=(Mz*Rz[np.ix_(p,p)])[mask].sum()
                    if v>cur+1e-9: cur=v;imp=True
                    else: p[[a,b]]=p[[b,a]]
        if cur>bestv: bestv=cur;bestp=p.copy()
    return bestv/mask.sum(),bestp
chs={'english':list(' etanroishldcumfpgwybvk'),'romaji':list(' aoiunktesrhmwdgyzbjcfp')}
txts={'english':eng,'romaji':rom}
rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
lab=lambda c:{' ':'_','\n':'$'}.get(c,c)
for mname,M in [('Mall',Mall),('Mlin',Mlin)]:
    for nm in chs:
        ch=chs[nm]; R=ref(txts[nm],ch)
        v,p=search(M,R,100)
        vf,pf=search(M,R,100,fix=(7,0))
        mp=[ch[i] for i in pf]
        print(mname,nm,'free best %.3f  (comp7->%s)  fixed comp7=space %.3f'%(v,lab(ch[p[7]]),vf))
        print('    ',''.join(lab(c) for c in mp))
        print('     next:',' '.join(f"{lab(mp[c])}:{''.join(lab(mp[x]) for x in np.argsort(-M[c])[:4])}" for c in range(23)))
        nulls=[]
        for k in range(8):
            pr=rng.permutation(23); pc=rng.permutation(23)
            nulls.append(search(M[pr][:,pc],R,25,seed=k)[0])
        print('     null (row+col shuffled, 25 restarts)',np.round(nulls,3),'t',round(time.time()-t0))

```python
import numpy as np, warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')
from sklearn.decomposition import FastICA
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=230)
Fraw=W['W_fc.0']; Pr=W['W_proj.0']; D2=W['ln2_w.0']; D1=W['ln1_w.0']
nr=np.linalg.norm(Fraw,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(Pr,axis=0)
idx=np.where(nr>1)[0]
F=Fraw[idx]
Oh=[W['W_o.0'][:,64*j:64*j+64] for j in range(6)]
stack=[]
for j,k in [(3,28),(5,24),(0,24),(1,16),(2,16),(4,16)]:
    stack.append(np.linalg.svd(Oh[j],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:k])
Bq,_=np.linalg.qr(D2[:,None]*np.hstack(stack))
Fr=F-F@Bq@Bq.T
U,S,Vt=np.linalg.svd(Fr,full_matrices=False)
Q3=W['W_q.0'][192:256]*D1; K3=W['W_k.0'][192:256]*D1
Pb=np.linalg.svd(np.vstack([Q3,K3]),full_matrices=False)[2][:30].T
fl=np.maximum(D2,0.15)
for r in [26,36,44]:
    best=None
    for seed in range(5):
        ica=FastICA(n_components=r,whiten='unit-variance',max_iter=2500,tol=2e-5,random_state=seed)
        Sx=ica.fit_transform(Fr@Vt[:r].T)
        kur=((Sx-Sx.mean(0))**4).mean(0)/(Sx.var(0)**2)-3
        if best is None or kur.sum()>best[0]: best=(kur.sum(),kur,Sx,ica.mixing_)
    _,kc,Sc,Mc=best
    sc=np.sign(((Sc-Sc.mean(0))**3).mean(0)); Sc=Sc*sc; Mc=Mc*sc
    o=np.argsort(-kc); kc=kc[o]; Sc=Sc[:,o]; Mc=Mc[:,o]
    Ac=Mc.T@Vt[:r]
    Tc=Ac/fl; tn=np.linalg.norm(Tc,axis=1); Tu=Tc/tn[:,None]
    Tm=Tu-Tu[:23].mean(0)
    print('r',r)
    print(' kurt ',kc.round(0))
    print(' n>3  ',(Sc>3).sum(0))
    print(' max z',Sc.max(0).round(0))
    print(' H3V  ',np.linalg.norm((W['W_v.0'][192:256]*D1)@Tm.T,axis=0).round(2))
    print(' H5V  ',np.linalg.norm((W['W_v.0'][320:384]*D1)@Tm.T,axis=0).round(2))
    print(' H3K  ',np.linalg.norm(K3@Tm.T,axis=0).round(2))
    print(' L3H3V',np.linalg.norm((W['W_v.3'][192:256]*W['ln1_w.3'])@Tm.T,axis=0).round(2))
    print(' inP  ',np.linalg.norm(Tu@Pb,axis=1).round(2))
    # output strength of the neurons of that comp
    pn=np.linalg.norm(Pr[:,idx],axis=0)
    print(' strongest neuron |proj| mean',np.array([pn[Sc[:,c]>3].mean() if (Sc[:,c]>3).any() else 0 for c in range(r)]).round(1))
