to iu hanashi de aru shikashi sono touji wa nani to iu kangae mo nakatta kara betsudan osoroshii to mo omowanakatta tada kare no tanagokoro ni nosete suu to mochiagerareta toki nandaka fuwafuwa shita kanji ga atta bakari de aru aru hi no kuregata no koto de aru hitori no genin ga rashoumon no shita de amayami wo matte ita hiroi mon no shita ni wa kono otoko no hoka ni dare mo inai tada tokorodokoro niri no hageta ookina maruhashira ni kirigirisu ga ippiki tomatte iru rashoumon ga suzakuooji ni aru ijou wa kono otoko no hoka ni mo ameyami wo suru ichimegasa ya momieboshi ga mou nisannin wa arisou na mono de aru sore ga kono otoko no hoka ni wa dare mo inai watakushi wa sono hito wo tsune ni sensei to yonde ita dakara koko de mo tada sensei to kaku dake de honmyou wa uchiakenai kore wa seken wo habakaru enryo to iu yori mo sono hou ga watakushi ni totte shizen dakara de aru watakushi wa sono hito no kioku wo yobiokosu goto ni sugu sensei to iitaku naru fude wo tottemo kokoromochi wa onaji koto de aru kunizakai no nagai tonneru wo nukeru to yukiguni de atta yoru no soko ga shiroku natta shingoujo ni kisha ga tomatta mukaigawa no zaseki kara musume ga tatte kite shimamura no mae no garasumado wo otoshita yuki no reiki ga nagarekonda musume wa mado ippai ni nori dashite tooku e sakebu you ni eki no hito ni koe wo kaketa kare wa sore wo kiite mimi wo sumashita ano toki no koto wo omoidasu to ima de mo mune ga itaku naru haha wa daidokoro de asagohan no shitaku wo shinagara chiisana koe de uta wo utatte ita chichi wa mainichi hayaku kara kaisha e dekakete yoru osoku made kaette konakatta watashitachi wa kawa no hotori wo aruite yama no ue made nobotte itta soko kara wa machi no akari ga yoku mieta kare wa fushigi na kao wo shite watakushi wo mita sore kara shibaraku damatte ita ga yagate chiisana koe de nanika itta keredomo watakushi ni wa yoku kikoenakatta sono ban wa ame ga hageshiku futte ita shikashi kanojo wa kasa mo sasazu ni soto e dete itta hito no kokoro to iu mono wa makoto ni fushigi na mono de aru toshi wo totta rouba ga hitori shizuka ni suwatte ita omae wa doko kara kita no ka to kiku to otoko wa nani mo kotaezu ni tada mugon de atama wo sageta"""
```python
eng="""the history of the city began in the early nineteenth century when settlers from the eastern states arrived in the valley the first railway line was completed in the following decade and connected the town with the coast which led to a rapid growth of population and trade during the war the factory produced aircraft parts and many residents served in the army after the war the university was founded by the state government and it has since become one of the largest research institutions in the country the album was released in the united kingdom by the record label and received generally positive reviews from music critics it reached number five on the national chart and was certified gold the band toured extensively in support of the album playing shows across europe and north america the species is found in tropical forests of south america where it feeds mainly on insects and small fruit adults are about twenty centimetres long with a dark brown back and a pale yellow belly the female lays two or three eggs in a nest built on a high branch and both parents take care of the young he was born in a small village near the river and was educated at the local school before moving to the capital to study law in his early career he worked as a journalist and later entered politics as a member of the national assembly he served as minister of finance for four years and was known for his reforms of the tax system the company was established as a family business and expanded quickly during the last decades of the century its headquarters are located in the city centre and it employs more than three thousand people in several countries the team won the championship in the first season under the new coach and qualified for the international competition the next year the game was developed by a small studio and published for several platforms and the player controls a character who must explore a large open world while solving puzzles and fighting enemies the church was built in the thirteenth century and is one of the oldest buildings in the region with a tall tower and a richly decorated interior that attracts many visitors every year according to the census the population of the district was about seven thousand most of whom live in rural areas and work in agriculture or fishing the river flows through the northern part of the province before joining the main stream near the border"""
def dc(M):
    M=M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True); M=M-M.mean(0,keepdims=True); M=M/M.std(0,keepdims=True); M=M-M.mean(1,keepdims=True); return M/M.std(1,keepdims=True)
def ref(t,ch,eps=0.3):
    i={c:k for k,c in enumerate(ch)}
    B=np.ones((len(ch),len(ch)))*eps
    for a,b in zip(t,t[1:]):
        if a in i and b in i: B[i[a],i[b]]+=1
    P=B/B.sum(1,keepdims=True)
    return np.log(P)
for nm,t,ch in [('english',eng,list(' etanroishldcumfpgwybvk')),('romaji',rom,list(' aoiunktesrhmwdgyzbjcfp'))]:
    R=dc(ref(t,ch))
    wr,vr=np.linalg.eigh(np.corrcoef(R)); wc,vc=np.linalg.eigh(np.corrcoef(R.T))
    v1=vr[:,-1]*np.sign(skew(vr[:,-1])); c1=vc[:,-1]*np.sign(skew(vc[:,-1]))
    print(nm,'row axis1 sorted',np.sort(v1).round(2),'skew %.2f'%skew(v1))
    print('   labels',''.join('_' if ch[i]==' ' else ch[i] for i in np.argsort(v1)))
    print(nm,'col axis1 sorted',np.sort(c1).round(2),'skew %.2f'%skew(c1))
    print('   labels',''.join('_' if ch[i]==' ' else ch[i] for i in np.argsort(c1)))
    print('   diag',np.diag(R).round(1)); print('   entries skew %.2f'%skew(R.ravel()))

print('model row axis sorted',np.sort(mr),'skew %.2f'%skew(mr)); print('model col axis sorted',np.sort(mc),'skew %.2f'%skew(mc))
```

```python
def fit(fr):
    f=np.sort(np.array(list(fr.values())))[::-1][:27]
    lf=np.log(f); ls=np.log(obs)
    A=np.c_[lf,np.ones(27)]
    co,res,_,_=np.linalg.lstsq(A,ls,rcond=None)
    pred=A@co
    return co[0],np.sqrt(((pred-ls)**2).mean()),np.exp(pred).round(1)
for nm,fr in [('english',eng),('python',pyt),('romaji',rom)]:
    a,rm,p=fit(fr); print(nm,'alpha %.2f rms log err %.3f'%(a,rm)); print('   pred',p)
print('obs',obs)
# restricted to dims 0..23 (strong part only)
for nm,fr in [('english',eng),('python',pyt),('romaji',rom)]:
    f=np.sort(np.array(list(fr.values())))[::-1][:24]
    A=np.c_[np.log(f),np.ones(24)]; ls=np.log(obs[:24])
    co=np.linalg.lstsq(A,ls,rcond=None)[0]
    print(nm,'strong only alpha %.2f rms %.3f'%(co[0],np.sqrt(((A@co-ls)**2).mean())))
```

```python
def dc(M):

    return np.log(P)
for nm,t,ch in [('english',eng,list(' etanroishldcumfpgwybvk')),('romaji',rom,list(' aoiunktesrhmwdgyzbjcfp'))]:
    R=dc(ref(t,ch)); V=[ch.index(c) for c in 'aeiou']; S=0; C=[i for i in range(23) if i not in V+[0]]
    print(nm,'row space: vowels %.2f others %.2f | col space: from vowels %.2f from others %.2f'%(R[0,V].mean(),R[0,C].mean(),R[V,0].mean(),R[C,0].mean()))
    print('   V->V %.2f V->C %.2f C->V %.2f C->C %.2f self-diag mean %.2f'%(R[np.ix_(V,V)].mean(),R[np.ix_(V,C)].mean(),R[np.ix_(C,V)].mean(),R[np.ix_(C,C)].mean(),np.diag(R).mean()))
    print('   sorted col space',np.sort(R[:,0])[::-1].round(1))
    print('   sorted row space',np.sort(R[0])[::-1].round(1))
print('model: row7 V5 -0.68 others 0.24; col7 from V5', np.mean([.3,-.3,1.5,.7,1.9]).round(2),'; V->V -0.86 V->other 0.24 C->V 0.19 C->C -0.05')
```

```python
import numpy as np
np.set_printoptions(precision=2, suppress=True, linewidth=200)
dims=[21,39,58,99]
Uo=np.linalg.svd(W['W_o.0'],full_matrices=False)[0][:,:2]
for l in [1,2,3]:
    w=W[f'ln1_w.{l}']; b=W[f'ln1_b.{l}']
    print('layer',l,'ln1_w at dims',w[dims],'ln1_b at dims',b[dims])
    gs=[]
    for h in range(6):
        s=slice(64*h,64*h+64)
        Qraw=W[f'W_q.{l}'][s]; Kraw=W[f'W_k.{l}'][s]
        KP=Kraw[:,dims]*w[dims]          # 64 x 4 : key response to the pointer dims
        q0=Qraw@b                        # constant part of the query
        g=KP.T@q0/8                      # constant-query slope on the 4 dims
        Qv=Qraw*w
        Bm=(Qv.T@KP)/8                   # 384 x 4 : variable-query coupling
        sB=np.linalg.svd(Bm,compute_uv=False)
        gs.append(g)
        A=Qv.T@(Kraw*w)/8
        Anp=A.copy(); Anp[:,dims]=0
        print(f' H{h}: |K on dims| {np.linalg.norm(KP):.1f} |q0| {np.linalg.norm(q0):.1f} slope g {g}  |g| {np.linalg.norm(g):.2f}; variable coupling sv {sB[:3]}; rest-of-A fro {np.linalg.norm(Anp):.2f} top sv {np.linalg.svd(Anp,compute_uv=False)[:3]}')
    G=np.array(gs); Gn=G/np.linalg.norm(G,axis=1,keepdims=True)
    print(' cos between head slopes'); print(Gn@Gn.T)
```
